## Add your Publication

If you have a publication related to the MOHID Modelling System and you want to include it in the MOHID bibliography list, please let us know by sending an email to [applications@mohid.com](mailto:applications@mohid.com) with the reference and the link where it can be found. Thank you very much for your collaboration.
